### 13 Nov 2026: build system replaced by the simple system (owner request)

- What: docs/ECONOMY-MODE.md rewritten from 230 lines and 13 sections to 95 lines (causes with evidence, roles, the 4-step path of a slice, 8 rules, hourly pulse, briefs, guards). Opus manager follows the plan and never builds; each Sonnet builder owns one whole slice to merged; Opus gate 2 once; Fable only for the design check and the final look; no Haiku in the build. docs/BUILD-STATE.md rewritten as one table with milestones (200 lines to about 95). New read-only script scripts/build-pulse.sh prints PROGRESS or STALL for the hourly check.
- Why: in 19 hours of economy mode the pace fell from 13 to 16 active slices per 3 hours to 4 to 6, 1 CI run in 12 was green, the same slices failed gate 3 up to 4 times, the manager restarted 7 times, and no usable preview reached the owner.
- Unchanged: rules/quality.md, the Definition of Done, all tests, the test guard, the push guard.
- Files: docs/ECONOMY-MODE.md, docs/BUILD-STATE.md, CLAUDE.md, rules/building.md, rules/sessions.md, docs/INDEX.md, docs/design/token-efficiency.md, docs/DECISIONS.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md, scripts/build-pulse.sh, .claude/agents/economy-orchestrator.md, economy-builder.md, economy-gate2.md, economy-gate3.md (rewritten), economy-mechanic.md and economy-search.md (removed). ### 13 Nov 2026: push guard hook narrowed (owner-approved)

- What: the Claude hook that blocks bypasses now lets read-only commands through (`git config --get` with or without a trailing command, `git config` with no value, grep and cat). It still blocks push or commit with the skip flag, setting or unsetting the hooks path, `-c` hooks-path overrides, and commands that delete, move or overwrite `.githooks/` or `scripts/push-guard.mjs`.
- Why: the old hook blocked harmless reads that only mentioned the setting, and did not block deleting the guard files.
- Files: .claude/hooks/push-guard-noverify.mjs, docs/CHANGELOG.md. Trials: 10 blocked and 10 allowed cases, all as expected.

### 13 Nov 2026: push guard (quick checks before every push)

- What: a pre-push hook in every worktree (core.hooksPath points at main's `.githooks`) runs Prettier and check:links on every branch, plus ESLint, tsc, check:ratchet, related Vitest and touched e2e specs (chromium, max 6, foundationInput smoke on build/next) on build/next and slice branches. A Claude hook blocks `--no-verify` and hooks-path changes; the only escape is a logged `push-guard-skip:` line.
- Why: most red CI runs were Prettier, check:links or one e2e spec, all catchable in seconds before pushing. No test, timeout or CI file changed.
- Files: scripts/push-guard.mjs, .githooks/pre-push, .gitattributes, .claude/hooks/push-guard-noverify.mjs, .claude/settings.json, .claude/agents/economy-builder.md, docs/ECONOMY-MODE.md, docs/CHANGELOG.md.

Text box v2: keep the layout engine, rebuild the touch layer. Plan only, no app code.

## 2026-11-12 (Triple tap in a box picked the whole paragraph on iPad)

- **What:** the visual-line measure looks through overlays (a box's resize handles) at the row edges while it measures.
- **Why:** WebKit hit the nw resize handle at the box edge, so both row edges came back as the same spot and three taps selected the whole paragraph (CI ipad-webkit boxTaps). App bug. TEMP CI debug logging removed.
- **Files:** src/editor/tapSelect.ts, e2e/boxTaps.spec.ts, e2e/toggles.spec.ts, docs/CHANGELOG.md.

## 2026-11-12 (Ribbon tools slid 6 px when the start chevron appeared)

- **What:** the scrolling rows (ribbon, context strip) always keep both zero-width chevron holders; only the chevron inside comes and goes.
- **Why:** a holder that appeared added one flex gap, so every tool slid 6 px just after the row scrolled; a quick next tap could miss (CI chromium newLine :76; failed 3 of 10 runs locally, 10 of 10 pass now). App bug.
- **Files:** src/app/tools/ScrollFade.tsx, docs/CHANGELOG.md.

## 2026-11-12 (CI: toolBarAlways test lost the sign-in gate switch)

- **What:** the "iPad that says it is a Mac" test sets its own storage, which replaced the config's `accountGate.off`; it now sets that key too.
- **Why:** since accounts became required (owner, 9 Nov) a device without sign-in is read-only, so the new notebook could not be renamed and the test timed out. Test out of date, not an app bug.
- **Files:** e2e/toolBarAlways.spec.ts, docs/CHANGELOG.md.

## 2026-11-11 (Lists no longer slide sideways after a row goes)

- **What:** a list that has the scroll handle keeps its 24 px gutter (new `data-scroll-gutter`) even when it stops overflowing. Before, the hints re-checked lists 400 ms after a change and dropped the gutter, so every row slid 24 px right just after a remove, and a quick next tap (Edit toolbar: Remove Eraser right after Remove Pen) landed in the gap and did nothing.
- **Why:** CI ipad-webkit editToolbar :105 (Eraser stayed, so Text could still be removed; the trace shows the gutter drop). App bug; new chromium test fails without the fix (445 vs 469 px).
- **Files:** src/app/scrollHints.ts, src/index.css, e2e/editToolbar.spec.ts, docs.

## 2026-11-11 (penUi test: button scrolled under the page bar on WebKit)

- **What:** the in-page button test scrolls its button into view before checking it is on top.
- **Why:** CI ipad-webkit penUi :85: focusing the editor scrolled the page 308 px (trace), so the button prepended above the text sat under the page bar and was never hit. Test setup, not an app bug; the checks are unchanged.
- **Files:** e2e/penUi.spec.ts, docs/CHANGELOG.md.

## 2026-11-11 (Lasso triple tap in a box selected the paragraph on WebKit)

- **What:** under the Lasso the ink pad covers the words; the tap counter looked through it to find the tap's spot but not to measure the line's left and right edges, so on WebKit (iPad) the edges hit the pad, the line could not be measured and three taps selected the whole paragraph. Both hit tests now look through the pad.
- **Why:** CI ipad-webkit boxTaps :107 and :138 (got the paragraph instead of the line). The earlier fix b56c056 (native multi-click) was not the cause. Minimal fix in tapSelect.ts with the owner's redesign in mind.
- **Files:** src/editor/tapSelect.ts, docs/CHANGELOG.md.

## 2026-11-11 (CI: rowReorder test waited too little)

- **What:** the re-order test now waits until each new row's title is saved before adding the next, and polls the first order check.
- **Why:** CI run 43064395551 (chromium) read the order once while Gamma's title was still being saved (the trace shows Gamma a moment later). Test timing, not an app bug; no assertion loosened.
- **Files:** e2e/rowReorder.spec.ts, docs/CHANGELOG.md.

## 2026-11-13 (Connector A slices 3-4: device list + write tools)

- **What:** the local Claude connector (mcp/) can now write, and is a revocable device. New tools `create_notebook`, `add_page`, `append_to_page` (version-checked, end of page only), `add_cards`; no delete, no edit of existing text. Records are sealed with the shared crypto under device id `mcp-<pc>` with clock stamps and one audit line per write (no content). Scopes in `~/.learning-notes-mcp/config.json` (all on by default). Settings › Account › Connected computers lists the PC with Revoke; the database refuses a revoked session at once.
- **Why:** the owner asked for read and write from the start, and for a way to cut a PC off from the app.

## 2026-11-12 (Q7a: Calendar, Year view, drag to change date, re-order rows)

- **Calendar view:** an optional **End date column** makes a row span several days (lanes per week, so a span keeps its place; the title shows on its first day and on Mondays). "+ N more" is now a button that lists every row of the day (windowed, so a day of thousands of rows is fine). Today highlighted, Previous / Today / Next kept.
- **Year view:** third range (Month / Week / Year): 12 mini months, a dot on days with rows (stronger with more), today circled; tap a day (or a month name) to open that month. Previous / Next move by a year.
- **Drag to change the date:** drag a row's chip to another day (mouse; Pencil / pen at once; finger after holding it, moving sooner scrolls); the date changes (a multi-day row keeps its length), a toast offers **Undo** (one step per drag). Keyboard: focus a row, Space picks it up, arrows move it a day (left/right) or a week (up/down), Space drops it, Escape puts it back; screen-reader texts announce each step. Dates with a time keep their local clock time.
- **Re-order rows in Table and List:** a grip (six dots) on each row; drag it (mouse, Pencil, finger: the grip never scrolls), or Space + Up / Down + Space. Only while the view has no sort; inside a group a row moves within its group. One Undo per move. Lists auto-scroll while dragging near the edge.
- **First load:** the Calendar screens load only when a Calendar view is opened (LazyCalendar.tsx); first load 301,869 B gzipped (limit 320,000).
- **Tests:** vitest calendarSpans / calendarMove / reorderRows (UTC-safe date maths run under 4 time zones, 10,000-row speed); e2e/databaseCalendar.spec.ts (mouse, pen, finger, keyboard, Undo, Year, +N more) and e2e/rowReorder.spec.ts (Table + List, mouse, keyboard, touch, sort hides grips).
- **Files:** databases/views/{CalendarView,CalendarYear,CalendarDayList,LazyCalendar,DragGhost,RowGrip}.tsx, {calendarSpans,calendarMove,reorderRows,useDragGesture,useRowReorder,useCalendarSpans}.ts(x), TableView.tsx, ListView.tsx, viewTypes.ts, index.css, i18n/{enCal,hiCal,esCal}.ts (spread into enQ/hiQ/esQ), docs/features/database-views.md.

## 2026-11-11 (PDF menu: organise, combine, scan, searchable, export)

- **What:** A **PDF** button in the reader bar (PDF books only) opens a menu, all lazy-loaded (none of it is in the first download; first load 305 KB gzipped). (1) **Organise pages:** every page as a picture; pick pages, turn left or right, move earlier or later (or drag), delete, add a blank page, extract the picked pages as a new Library PDF; Save writes the new file. The book's highlights, notes, bookmarks, ink and reading place are moved, turned or removed in the same transaction (a failure leaves the book as it was), and one Undo in the toast puts the file and every mark back. (2) **Combine files:** this PDF and any PDFs from the device, reordered, become one new PDF in the Library; originals untouched. (3) **Scan to PDF:** take a photo (iPad camera) or pick pictures; the sheet is found on the device (largest bright area, four corners), straightened (perspective warp), with draggable corner handles (also moved by arrow keys) as the manual fallback; looks Colour, Grey, Document (paper white, text dark); multi-page; optional on-device text reading; the result is a new Library PDF. (4) **Make searchable (OCR):** Tesseract (tesseract.js 7, Apache-2.0) on the device, English, from files of our own site (worker and core are hashed assets, `public/ocr/eng.traineddata.gz`), nothing fetched from elsewhere, progress per page, Stop keeps what was read; writes an invisible text layer so the PDF can be searched, selected and copied. An optional **AI reading** (handwriting, poor scans) shows the cost first and goes through `callAi`, so AI off, the notebook switch, locked pages and the monthly limit all apply. (5) **Export with my marks:** highlights, notes (numbered markers plus a Notes page at the end), ink and the highlighter flattened onto the pages; **Export clean copy:** the file without them. Both download. Pen, Highlighter, Note and Text were not changed.
- **Why:** Owner decision Q8 (Acrobat-style PDF tools, no Fill & Sign).
- **Tests:** src/reader/pdfTools/pageOps.test.ts (17: plan, anchors, rectangles, ink turn maths, reading place, blank size), pdfEdit.test.ts (13: reorder/delete/turn/blank, combine, extract, pictures to PDF, coordinates for every page turn, text layer, flatten, Undo restores file/highlights/ink, rollback on failure), scanGeometry.test.ts (10: sheet found upright and tilted, no sheet, homography, straightened page, looks), aiOcr.test.ts (3: cost, clean, AI off sends nothing); e2e/pdfTools.spec.ts (8 in Chromium: menu, organise with highlight and Undo, ink follows its page and goes with a deleted page, rotate/blank/last page, combine, export with and without marks, scan, scan with real on-device OCR then Find).
- **Files:** src/reader/pdfTools/*, src/reader/BookReader.tsx (the PDF button, menu, lazy host, `key` to redraw), src/i18n/{enPdf,esPdf,hiPdf}.ts and en/es/hi.ts, src/ai/usageReport.ts (`pdfOcr` counts as Convert to text), vite.config.ts (the 4 MB text-reader core is not precached), public/ocr/, package.json (pdf-lib 1.17.1, tesseract.js 7.0.0), e2e/pdfTools.spec.ts, docs/features/library.md.

## 2026-11-11 (Drive status: the line now tells the server's truth)

- **Root cause of "0 rows" with a line that said otherwise:** the "Drive: stays connected / will ask" line, and the choice to show Google's consent screen, read a flag kept only in this browser (`sync.driveOffline`). The flag was set in one place only (a Google sign-in whose reply carried Google's refresh token and whose store call was accepted) and cleared in another (the server said none/revoked). Every other case left it wrong in either direction: a device that signed in another way or lost its browser data said "will ask" while the server held the key and was renewing the pass fine; a device that once stored the key kept saying "stays connected" after the row was gone; an account that signs in only by email never has a Google refresh token, so its table has 0 rows by design. Nothing could correct the flag, and nothing could show what the server really held. Why a particular Google sign-in sends no refresh token (Google sends one only after a consent screen; Supabase passes it on only then) cannot be proven without the live project: the slow-moments `drive` line still names that case.
- **Fix:** the Drive function gets a fifth answer, `status` (is a key kept: yes or no, never the key). `checkDriveLasting` (Settings › Account opens it) asks the server and corrects this device's flag; offline or a missing function change nothing. A pass that comes back from the server also sets the flag. Tests: src/sync/connectors/driveServer.test.ts (server says no key while the device remembers one; server has it while the device forgot; pass from the server; offline and missing function change nothing; the status handler answers only the owner and never returns the key).
- **Owner step:** redeploy the function once so it knows `status` (until then the old behaviour stays, nothing breaks).
- **Files:** supabase/functions/_shared/driveTokenHandler.ts, src/sync/connectors/{driveServer,supabase,types}.ts, src/sync/{syncService.ts,AccountSection.tsx}.

## 2026-11-11 (Sync: reading progress and favourite stars)

- **What:** Where a book was last read (progress, spot, page count, furthest place) and the favourite star now follow the account. Both live on the page record (a book's `source` and the new `Page.favourite = {on, at}`), which already syncs; a new small merge rule for `pages` (`mergePageReading` in `src/sync/merge.ts`) puts the two sides' reading place and star together when both devices changed the page, instead of one side losing everything: the place comes from the device the book was opened on most recently, the furthest place is the larger, the star is the newest. A person's own choice after signing back in still wins as before. The engine saves the merged page and sends it again. `enqueueChanged` now also finds a star or a read made while sync was off (they do not bump `updatedAt`). Favourites keep their instant device list (`app/favourites.ts`); `app/favouriteSync.ts` writes older local stars to their pages once and follows stars that arrive (lazy chunk, after the database opens). Backups accept the new field.
- **Why:** Reading progress and favourites were per device (known gap in sync.md); the owner reads on the iPad and the PC.
- **Tests:** src/sync/readingSync.test.ts (17: two devices offline, furthest kept, title edit not undone, found after sync was off, star both ways, two stars at once, list rebuild, start-up migration).
- **Files:** src/sync/{merge,engine}.ts, src/core/types.ts, src/share/checkRows.ts, src/app/{favourites,favouriteSync}.ts, src/main.tsx, docs/features/sync.md.

## 2026-11-11 (AI cost audit)

- **Money safety in `callAi`:** AI-off checked live inside it; running calls reserve their worst-case cost against the key's cap; identical overlapping requests share one paid call; a call that could cost over US$0.10 needs the screen's shown cost (`approvedUsd`, passed by prompts, skills, fact check, clean-up, Ask book, mind-map rebuild); a timeout is counted and never sent to another model; a stream that drops keeps and counts its words; Test key obeys the cap and a double tap makes one call; with key sync on, devices share one monthly cap through per-device `aiUsage` records. ## 2026-11-11 (Reliability: flaky specs)

- **App cause found: a link opened while the app starts was overwritten.** The route effect in App.tsx replaced the address with the screen the app began on (Home) even when the address had changed since the first draw (about 1 start in 8 after a reload). It now follows the new address. This was the random failure of `aiUsage` (monthly limit, Connections) and other specs that reload and then open a settings page. Regression: e2e/bootHash.spec.ts (8 reloads; failed 2 of 3 runs before the fix, 40 of 40 clean runs of the old aiUsage steps after).

- **State-based waits instead of pauses:** e2e `waitForSaved(page)` (helpers.ts) waits until the app says no page edit is waiting to be written (`window.__lnTools.pendingSaves`, app/toolsDebug.ts), replacing the 800/900 ms pauses after typing in `typeInEditor`, readingHelpers, reading.spec and revise.spec. `selectText` now waits for the browser's selectionchange (and a frame) so a button clicked at once acts on the new selection. Unit tests that slept 5 to 100 ms for sync or a live query (keySync, settingsMirror, settingsMigration, costCurrency, aiCost) now `vi.waitFor` the result. No assertion was weakened or removed.

## 2026-11-12 (Security: low findings)

- **Pencil test page out of production:** moved from `public/` to `dev/pencil-test/`; served only by `npm run dev` and by the E2E preview (E2E=1) through a Vite plugin, never built or deployed. The worker's special rules for it were removed. (The owner's old iPad link /pencil-test.html no longer exists on the live site; run it from the dev server.)
- **HSTS:** `includeSubDomains` added (no preload).
- **Service worker checked:** it is generated with precache only (no runtime caching), so it stores only the build's own same-origin files and never an opaque or cross-origin response. A guard test (scripts/siteRules.test.mjs) fails if `runtimeCaching` is ever added.
- **Import paths checked:** EPUB renders in script-less sandboxed frames; PDF in canvas; Markdown/NotebookLM/Notion create text and `lnimg:` pictures only; the paste/DOCX sanitizer drops svg, math, video, audio, iframes and `data:image/svg+xml`; stored pictures accept PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF only, so an SVG is never shown as markup (Notion SVG files become download-only File blocks).
- **Files:** vite.config.ts, public/_headers, dev/pencil-test/*, e2e/pencilTest.spec.ts, scripts/siteRules.test.mjs.

## 2026-11-12 (Security: remote images)

- **Web pictures never load silently:** the editor's `resolveFileUrl` now returns a small "Load remote image" card (a data: SVG, loads nothing) for any http(s) picture not approved this visit. A tap copies the picture into the app if its server allows it (image element with CORS, drawn to a bitmap, so it works under the CSP and an SVG is never kept as markup), otherwise loads it from the web for this visit only; a failure puts the card back with a toast. On paste (HTML and Markdown), https pictures are copied first, at most 12 and a 4 s wait; the rest show the card. A paste with no web pictures lands at once as before. Copying a page/picture never fetches a picture still behind its card.
- **Checked, no change needed:** Notion import keeps remote media as text links (never image blocks); Markdown/NotebookLM/Anki/capture imports only create `lnimg:` pictures; the paste sanitizer already drops svg/video/audio/iframe and `data:image/svg+xml`; DOCX pages pass through the same sanitizer. Not done: remote pictures inside EPUB chapters (see DECISIONS).
- **Files:** src/images/remoteImages.ts (+test), src/editor/{PageEditor.tsx,pasteFlow.ts,clipboard.ts,copyFormats.ts}, src/i18n/{en8,es8,hi8}.ts, e2e/remoteImages.spec.ts.

## 2026-11-12 (Security: Lock tries)

Before it was memory only and 5 free tries. The wait shows minutes once it is 2 min or more.

## 2026-11-12 (iPad WebKit: multi-tap select, penUi, paperInk rule 7)

- **Multi-tap select (root cause):** WebKit selects the paragraph itself on the third mousedown and ProseMirror does the same on the third mouseup/click; both ran around our selection and won (213 chars). `editor/tapSelect.ts` now cancels (preventDefault + stopImmediatePropagation, capture) the mousedown, mouseup, click and dblclick of a tap that is the 2nd or later of a run, for 700 ms, so only our counter selects. Specs: boxTaps compares the triple-tap selection with the actual drawn row of the tap (WebKit wraps narrower, 16 chars is a valid row), not a fixed 20.
- **penUi (WebKit):** no app fault found by reading (penTap clicks `[data-ui]` on a Pencil lift). The spec tapped before the page hold ended and without checking what is on top; it now waits and polls the hit target. Unit test added for a data-ui button inside an editable box.
- **paperInk rule 7:** `getByText(...).click()` timed out in WebKit; the spec now clicks the line by its measured box.
- **Files:** src/editor/tapSelect.ts, src/app/penTap.test.ts, e2e/{boxTaps,penUi,paperInk}.spec.ts.

## 2026-11-12 (Text tool + Pencil: safer shapes, Undo fix)

- **Undo fix:** every box has its own ink controller listening to Ctrl/Cmd+Z on the document, and each one ran Undo, so with two or more boxes one press took back several steps (a circled number's Undo also took back the typing). Now the first one stops the key (`stopImmediatePropagation`). Files: `ink/controller.ts`.
- **Shapes no longer misread handwriting:** the Text tool converts a stroke only when its fit score is 0.55 or more, it is big enough (line/arrow 60 px, rectangle/circle 48 px across), no other pen stroke was made within 1.2 s and 80 px, and no nearby stroke starts within 0.4 s after the lift (the shape waits that long). A circle round a digit is immediate and keeps its own check (the circle must hug a 0 to 20 number). A number inside a longer number (2024) is never circled. Files: `paper/textPencil.ts`, `paper/PaperFrame.tsx`. Tests: `paper/textPencil.test.ts`, `e2e/textBoxFree.spec.ts` (chromium and ipad-webkit).
- Checked by reading, not changed: iPad Scribble is not blocked by our guards. The Pencil-hold, double-tap and stylus-touch guards in `editor/tapSelect.ts`, `holdSelect.ts` and `app/penTap.ts` act only on second taps, on a hold, or on our own chrome; `inputmode="none"` only hides the on-screen keyboard. Needs the owner's iPad check.

## 2026-11-11 (Sign-in stays, owner bug)

- **Root cause:** the sign-in renewal (refresh token, single-use) ran several times at once on start (sync, live feed, Drive, key and layout sync all ask for a token) and treated any plain HTTP 400 as "session ended". After the access token expired (an hour or more closed, or after an update), the twin request got a 400 "already used", the saved sign-in was deleted and the "signed out" lock / sign-in asks came back. An odd 400 (proxy) or an offline sign-in callback could also wipe it.
- **Fix:** one renewal at a time (in-page promise plus Web Locks across tabs); a newer session another tab stored is adopted; the session ends only on 401/403/404 or a 400 with a dead-token code (`sessionEnded(status, body)`); a failed callback while offline no longer signs out; the app renews quietly on focus and pageshow, and with a sync on visible and online.
- Tests: `sessionEnd.test.ts` (twin renewals, adopt other tab, days offline, odd 400), `e2e/staySignedIn.spec.ts`.
- Files: src/sync/connectors/supabase.ts, sessionEnd.ts, src/sync/syncService.ts, tests.

## 2026-11-11 (Pencil double tap erased the word)

- **Bug (owner, iPad):** a Pencil double tap on a word, then the word vanished. Cause: under the Text tool Scribble is free, so the data-loss guard let small deletes and writes through; a little Pencil drift or a stray Scribble dot right after the tap replaced or deleted the freshly selected word. Fix: a 600 ms grace period after a tap selects text (word, line or paragraph): every delete, replace or write (beforeinput and transaction level) is refused unless a real key was pressed, whatever the Scribble setting. The grace protects only the selected range (a press on a bar or menu ends it at once, so Delete/Cut still work; a synced change to another block still applies; gestureWatchdog R7-4 caught this). Files: src/editor/penGuard.ts (SELECT_GRACE_MS, noteTapSelected, selectGraceOn), src/editor/tapSelect.ts, tests in src/editor/penGuard.test.ts and e2e/pencilTaps.spec.ts.

- **Flaky test fixed:** driveServer.test.ts checked that random ciphertext did not contain the two letters "RT" (failed by chance in CI); it now uses a long marker.

## 2026-11-10 (Builder D: one Undo history per page (3), one Undo/Redo place (35))

- **Item 3:** every action now feeds one ordered list per page (scope `page:<id>`, 200 steps). Text in every box is a marker per typing group (the box's own ProseMirror history, 300 deep, 800 ms pause, registered as the `yUndo` extension so `editor.undo()`, Ctrl+Z and the iOS shake all reach the journal); ink (stroke, erase, Lasso move or delete) is a step per gesture; a delete of ink and text is one step (`chainLast`). Database writes are recorded **from the database itself** (Dexie hooks, `core/rowCapture.ts`): page settings (paper style, size, locks, title, tabs), boxes and the grid rows, tags, databases and decks (not cards or block tags: they follow the lines); a transaction is one step, quick repeats of the same field merge (typing a title). Box add/remove/copy take the lines in the box with them. Restore of a version is one step (undo restores the "before" copy). API (`core/historySteps.ts`, tiny, first download): `pushStep`, `group`, `transaction`, `undo`, `redo`, `quietly`/`quietAsync`, `undoSince`, `registerKind`, `attachEditor`, `releaseOwner`, `setActiveScope`. The journal (`history/pageHistory.ts`) loads in the background. Steps with data (rows, restore) are saved per page in the `meta` table and survive a reload; text and ink steps last the session (their editors are rebuilt on reload). Undo never silently does nothing: nothing left, no page open and a step that cannot be applied each say why in a toast, and the bad step is dropped. The box toasts' Undo uses the journal when their action is the newest step (`undoSince`). Tests: `src/history/pageHistory.test.ts` (every table classified, undo/redo of rows, group order, 200 limit, error toast, saved over reload, box lines), `src/history/bypass.test.ts` (fails if code imports the ProseMirror history or the ink's raw undo outside the journal, or keeps another undo stack), `e2e/undoHistory.spec.ts` (typing, paper style and size, new box + its text + lock, delete box, drawing, the silent-nothing toast, 50 random steps back and forward). Files: src/core/{historySteps,rowCapture,rowTypes,db}.ts, src/history/{pageHistory,versionService}.ts, src/editor/{historyExtension,historyActions,PageEditor,pageSync,DockedBar}.ts(x), src/ink/{controller,InkSurface}.ts(x), src/paper/usePaperBoxes.tsx, src/pages/PageView.tsx, src/App.tsx, src/sync/outbox.ts, src/app/tools/toolRegistry.ts, i18n enO/esO/hiO.
- **Item 35:** one Undo/Redo icon pair (`app/tools/HistoryButtons.tsx`, `data-testid="history-undo"/"history-redo"`) at the far left of the PDF/EPUB reader bar and of the reading-mode bar; the main tool bar and ribbon already pin theirs at the far left and now read the same state; the words Undo and Redo in the book reader's Pen strip are gone; the Write pad uses the main bar's pair. e2e/bookInk.spec.ts checks the position; barsCrisp.spec.ts and bookInk.spec.ts use the new test ids.
- **Item 8 (restore as one step):** a version restore is one Undo step (see above).
- First download: 318.4 KB of 320 KB with everything merged (the journal, the database hooks, the editor extension and the English strings are lazy; only the small `core/historySteps.ts` facade is in the first load; the hooks are added when the history loads at start).

## 2026-11-10 (Tools robustness: stuck Lasso, Text, Pencil after use or an app switch)

Plan: [plans/tools-robustness-plan.md](plans/tools-robustness-plan.md). Research: [research/tools-robustness-2026-11.md](research/tools-robustness-2026-11.md). One bullet per step.

- **Step 1, one input state machine:** src/app/inputMachine.ts. A module that starts a gesture calls `begin`; a lift, a cancel, a lost pointer capture (document, capture phase), an app switch (watchdog), a tool change or the **4 s age limit** (pointer down, no movement) send it back to idle through one function and reset the owner. The Lasso and handle drags register with it. New reset reasons `timeout`, `toolchange`, `health`, `lost`. `window.__lnTools` (toolsDebug.ts) lets tests read the dump. **Files:** src/app/{inputMachine,inputMachine.test,toolsDebug,gestureReset}.ts, src/ink/controller.ts, src/main.tsx, e2e/{tools-machine.spec,toolsHelpers}.ts, playwright.config.ts.
- **Step 2, the Lasso on the machine:** a new touch no longer returns early when a Lasso or handle gesture is stuck: if the same pointer goes down again, or the machine no longer knows the old one, the old gesture is cancelled first. A lift of another pointer cancels a gesture the machine has dropped. A tool change cancels the gesture (machine). **Files:** src/ink/controller.ts, src/app/toolsDebug.ts (setLasso for tests), e2e/lassoLoops.spec.ts (20 loops: free and rectangle, ink, words, picture, ink over words; Esc, tap or nothing between; a swallowed lift then 5 loops; a lost lift then a new pointer id after 4.8 s).
- **Step 3, one owner of contenteditable:** src/editor/editLock.ts (`lock(owner, el)`, `unlock(owner)`, counted). The Lasso/handle gesture (controller), the Pencil text drag (penSelect) and the grip drag (gripDrag) no longer save and write back the attribute; the page editor gets `editableIn(pageMode)`, any other element what it had at the first lock. `applyPageMode` re-asserts held locks (`onModeApplied`); the watchdog clears all locks; `toolState().editLock` lists owners. A Pencil text drag registers with the input machine (4 s limit). **Files:** src/editor/{editLock,editLock.test,editPolicy,penSelect}.ts, src/ink/controller.ts, src/app/tools/gripDrag.ts, e2e/toolsEditLock.spec.ts.
- **Step 4, the remaining flags:** tapSelect listens for `pointercancel`. holdSelect, the selection gesture (`selectGesture`), ownSelection, penGuard, penTap, grip drag and the automatic device mode counter register with the machine, so a lift that never comes is dropped after 4 s without any app switch (and at once on a tool change). holdSelect clears its timer when the app is hidden (its watchdog reset). `toolState().listeners` counts the document and window listeners by event type and phase (src/app/listenerCount.ts). **Files:** src/editor/{tapSelect,holdSelect,gesture,dragSelect,ownSelection,penGuard}.ts, src/app/{penTap,listenerCount,inputMachine}.ts, src/app/tools/gripDrag.ts, src/settings/deviceModeSettings.ts, src/main.tsx, e2e/toolsFlags.spec.ts.
- **Step 5, health check:** src/app/healthCheck.ts. Runs on visible, pageshow, focus and every 10 s while idle (page showing, no pointer for 30 s). Checks: a registered "down" flag set while the machine is idle and quiet (heal: the gesture reset), a page whose `contenteditable` disagrees with `editableIn(mode)` and no lock is held (heal: set it), a lock with no gesture, the pad holding a capture with no gesture, ink canvases with a lost context or blank tiles that should show ink (heal: repaint all tiles; `InkRenderer.blankTiles/contextLost`, `InkController.repaintAll`), too many pointer listeners (heal: reset). It also reads the settings table again (`rereadSettings`) and looks for a new version (at most once per 2 min). Every problem and heal is in `toolState().health`. The same heal failing twice within 60 s calls the stuck handler (soft refresh, step 6). **Files:** src/app/{healthCheck,toolsDebug,inputMachine}.ts, src/ink/{controller,render}.ts, src/settings/settingsMirror.ts, src/main.tsx, e2e/toolsHealth.spec.ts.
- **Step 6, soft refresh:** src/app/softRefresh.ts. "Refresh app" row in the settings gear menu (Quiet corner): saves notes and ink (`flushAllSaves`, `flushAllInk`, 4 s cap), remembers {hash, pageId, scrollTop, tool, at} in sessionStorage and reloads; on start the scroll and tool come back once, if under 60 s old. Automatic: the health check calls it when the same heal fails twice within 60 s; it waits for a safe moment (machine idle, no composition, no sheet with typed input, nothing unsaved) with the updates safe-reload poll, at most once per 10 minutes, then toasts "App refreshed to fix a stuck tool". A heal that works later (ink repaint) is re-checked after 1.5 s, not at once. The health check, soft refresh and console hook load when the browser is idle (src/app/robustness.ts) to keep the first download under the limit. **Files:** src/app/{softRefresh,robustness,healthCheck,toolsDebug}.ts, src/ink/{inkDoc,controller}.ts, src/app/spaces/QuietCorner.tsx, src/App.tsx, src/main.tsx, src/i18n/{en,es,hi}Robust.ts (+ Nbh), e2e/toolsRefresh.spec.ts.
- **Step 7, resume suite:** e2e/resume.spec.ts: 10 Lasso loops, an app switch in the middle of a loop (with and without pointercancel), 10 more; after an app switch a Pencil tap places the caret, a double tap selects a word, a Pencil Text-filter loop selects words, the Pen draws and Text types; a half loop, Text chosen, app switch: typing works. All new specs are in the ipad-webkit list. Docs: features/ink.md, HANDOFF, owner-checks-pending.

## 2026-11-09 (Contextual tool bar, smooth picture resize, sheet-gap fixes)

- **What:** (1) Contextual strips like Word's contextual tabs: a selected picture, video or embed, table, drawing selection or words adds a tab (Picture, Video, Table, Drawing, Text) to the full tool bar, which opens on it and returns to the previous tab when the selection clears; the compact bar gets a row with the first three actions and More (opens the full bar on that tab). Picture strip: Smaller, Bigger, Fit width, Original size, resize handles, Crop and rotate, Replace, Align left/centre/right, Caption and alt text, Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Delete, Deselect. (2) A pasted or placed picture is selected at once. (3) Resize follows the finger every frame (one rAF write, will-change, pagination held), commit on release (one Undo); size presets glide 180 ms (instant with Reduce motion). (4) Bug "picture disappeared": width maths guarded (never 0, negative or NaN; minimum 48 px), the start size is measured fresh at every pointer-down, a drag is cancelled when the app goes to the background or the pointer is lost, bad saved widths are repaired on load. (5) Real cause of the owner's case: the sheet-gap rule pushed the too-big picture to the next sheet. Now: pagination is held while dragging, a dashed ghost shows where it will land, on release the picture slides (220 ms), the page scrolls to follow and it pulses; the blank end of the sheet shows "Continues on next page" and a tap there puts the caret at the end of the last block (or makes an empty line after a picture).
- **Why:** owner iPad feedback (9 Nov).

## 2026-11-09 (iPad: selection handles and repeated Lasso loops)

- **What:** the round selection handles (app/tools/main/MiniToolbar.tsx) now sit at the selection edge (start handle above-left, end handle below-right), remember where the finger took hold (grab offset) and move the edge by the finger's movement, so the first move never jumps a line; a no-change move is ignored; `dropNativeSelection` runs on every drag step (no iPadOS menu). Tests in e2e/lassoWords.spec.ts: handle nudge and drag, Copy/Cut/Undo from our pill, repeated loops (free and rectangle).

(2) The rectangle path already used the same word flow as the free-form one (verified by test); the "3rd loop does nothing" is the pill: it sits above the previous selection and its buttons (and the handles) lie over the pad, so a loop that starts under them never reaches the Lasso. Tapping empty paper or Esc closes the pill and the next loop works (tested, 5 in a row). Not changed: moving the pill away automatically (DECISIONS).

## 2026-11-09 (Remote sign-out: lock, choice, Start fresh)

- **What:** when another device uses "Sign out on all devices", this device learns it at its next renewal (the server refuses the refresh as invalid: 400/401/403/404; offline, 429 and 5xx are only "try later", a normal token expiry renews with a 200) and shows a full-screen glass lock "You were signed out. Sign in to continue" (modal dialog, solid backdrop: notes hidden, not erased, nothing behind it usable). Only Sign in and Start fresh work. The flag survives a reload. Signing back in with unsent edits opens a choice: Merge both (default, the normal two-device merge: both kept, other version in History, Keep both where lines differ), Use this device's version, Use the cloud version, with pages changed here and there; syncing waits for the answer; the unchosen version always goes to History. Start fresh: warning with the count of unsent changes, saves one last backup file, then removes this device's notes, key and settings and reloads into the welcome. Never-signed-in devices and "Use without an account" are unaffected.
- **Why:** owner-approved design (9 Nov): like Instagram, a remote sign-out must be visible and safe, and nothing may be lost.
- **Files:** src/sync/{SignedOutGate,SignedOutLock,SyncChoiceDialog}.tsx, remoteSignOut.ts, divergence.ts, syncService.ts, syncStatus.ts, engine.ts (SyncChoice, previewRemote), merge.ts (prefer), connectors/{sessionEnd,supabase,memory,types}.ts, src/App.tsx, src/i18n/{en,es,hi}Sync.ts; tests src/sync/remoteSignOut.test.ts, src/sync/connectors/sessionEnd.test.ts, e2e/remoteSignOut.spec.ts.

## 2026-11-09 (Update check fix)

- **What:** "Check for updates now" and the automatic look no longer depend on the service-worker registration. New `lookForUpdate` (src/app/updates.ts) reads /version.json (no-store, cache-busted), compares it with the built commit, also asks `registration.update()` when there is one (its errors are swallowed), and when a newer build exists with no service-worker update waiting, offers a fallback restart (drop service workers and caches, reload) that goes through the normal auto-update safe-moment rule. "Could not check" now shows only when neither version.json nor the service worker could be reached. Looks at start, on foreground, navigation, input and every 10 min now run even when registration failed. The auto-update backup step no longer blocks: a failing or slow (8 s) backup (large synced library) proceeds; only low storage or unsaved typing asks. sw.js and version.json were already no-cache in public/_headers.
- **Why:** owner stuck on an old build; update only ran after signing out of sync. The safety copy of a big synced library failed or hung, so the update question was raised once and then held back for the session.
- **Files:** src/app/{updates,autoUpdate}.ts, src/main.tsx, tests src/app/{updates,autoUpdate}.test.ts, e2e/versionCheck.spec.ts.

## 2026-11-09 (Design step 2: the frame)

- **What:** the app frame on the new look. A fixed violet wash behind everything (`bg-wash` on the shell); a floating glass sidebar (12 px gap, radius 16) and a flat-glass strip of spaces (the icon rail: hiding the sidebar leaves it); the top bar and tool bar share ONE glass layer; the page is a solid rounded card. Phones stay edge to edge (`--frame-gap` is 0). Active rows use the soft violet tint, section labels are 11 px caps, the Review badge uses on-accent. New `src/design/glass.ts`: Settings > General > Reduce glass (Automatic or Solid) and a frame-time probe that sets `data-glass="lite"` for the session when scrolling or pointer bursts run slower than 20 ms.

## 2026-11-09 (Drive lasting connection: visible status and diagnosis)

- **What:** Live finding: after sign-out/in with the consent screen the server had 0 rows and the function saw no request. Code check: every sign-in (Settings, SignInGuide, welcome, Reconnect bar) goes through one path, PKCE `finishSignIn`; the store call is made only when the token reply carries `provider_refresh_token`, and Supabase (auth source token.go) copies it into the reply only when Google handed one over. No request means the reply had none; the call itself is awaited, the URL is the project URL, and nothing else skips it. The old code logged nothing in that case. Now: the case is logged (`no Google refresh token`, store status) in the slow-moments log (Settings > Advanced, kind `drive`, no secrets), and Settings > Sync shows "Drive: stays connected" or "Drive: will ask to reconnect (tap to fix)" with the last note and a "Set up lasting connection" button that signs in again with the consent screen. Unit tests: PKCE sign-in stores the token with the new JWT, no token logged, refused store logged without secret.
- **Why:** owner could not tell whether the lasting connection was set up.
- **Files:** src/sync/connectors/{driveDiag,driveServer,supabase,types}.ts, driveServer.test.ts, src/sync/{syncService,SyncSection}.ts(x), src/perf/slowMoments.ts, src/i18n/{en,es,hi}Sync.ts, docs/{HANDOFF,CHANGELOG}.md, docs/plans/owner-checks-pending.md

## 2026-11-09 (Ink scrolls natively: overlay inside the scroller, tiled canvases)

- **What:** the ink overlay now sits inside the page scroller (child of a zero-size positioned anchor at the end of the editor container; it was positioned from the body and clipped and re-placed by script on every scroll). Finished ink is on 640 px tiles (`ink` and `hl` canvases only where strokes touch; at most 8 reusable slots per box, moved by transform, spare ones given back when idle); one screen-sized `live` canvas holds only the stroke being drawn, eraser ring, lasso and peeled tape. Scrolling runs no ink code: tiles are chosen again after 160 px from cached geometry, and once with fresh geometry 150 ms after the scroll stops. Removed: per-scroll measuring, re-placing, clip-path, bitmap-per-layer cache, the rubber-band tracker and the visual-viewport listeners (the overlay can no longer land over the bars: the scroller clips it). Also fixed: under a zoomed paper (CSS zoom) the overlay was sized in zoomed px (1.56x too wide in Chromium at 156 %); it is now zoomed back by the measured ratio. The mixed paper page at 4x CPU: late frames 17-25 % -> 0.9 % (73 %), 1.0 % (100 %), 3.0 % (150 %); script per frame 2.5-4.0 -> 0.7 ms; long tasks 145 -> 6. 1x CPU: 0 %.
- **Why:** owner stutter on big pages and the iPad ink drifting over the tool bar on overscroll (9 Nov): both come from the overlay living outside the scroller. Research: docs/research/perf-big-pages-2026-11.md (third pass).
- **Files:** src/ink/render.ts, src/ink/controller.ts, e2e/inkScrollNative.spec.ts (new), e2e/inkHelpers.ts (painted, paintedBox, inkClientBox join the tiles), e2e/bookInk.spec.ts, magnifier.spec.ts, paperInk.spec.ts, inkOnPicture.spec.ts (Text tool before clicking the editor; root instead of canvas), e2e/lassoPicture.spec.ts (force click: the Lasso pad covers the editor), playwright.config.ts (inkScrollNative on the iPad stand-in), e2e/bigPageBudget.spec.ts (budget 10 % -> 2 % late at 1x, 5 % at 4x, slots <= 8), docs/features/ink.md, docs/research/perf-big-pages-2026-11.md, docs/DECISIONS.md, docs/HANDOFF.md, docs/plans/owner-checks-pending.md

## 2026-11-09 (Scroll trace of a mixed paper page)

- **What:** measured scrolling on a paper page with 20 pictures, embeds, tables, 12 boxes and 600 ink strokes at 73 %, 100 % and 150 % zoom, at 1x and 4x CPU. 1x: 0 % late frames, 0.4 ms script per frame. 4x: 9-17 % late frames, 2-3 ms script per frame; the cause is the ink layer being measured, re-placed, re-clipped and repainted on every scroll event (off: 0 % late frames). Pictures, embeds, box frames, snap guides and calm handles did not show. No code fix kept: three attempts halved the ink redraw's CPU but not the late frames and broke four ink specs, so they were reverted; the real fix (ink overlay inside the scroller) is a separate step. New CI budget for this page in `e2e/bigPageBudget.spec.ts`.
- **Why:** owner saw stutter while scrolling a big pasted document.
- **Files:** e2e/bigPageBudget.spec.ts, docs/research/perf-big-pages-2026-11.md.

## 2026-11-09 (Speed on big pages: typing and paste)

- **What:** measured a generated page of 2,000 blocks / 156 sheets (Chromium, also with the CPU slowed 4x). Typing cost 11-15 ms of script per key (45 ms at 4x), 50 long tasks at 4x. Causes and fixes: the sheet-gap pass asked the DOM for every block's document position on every key (now read from the document in one pass); table row heights looked up every table by id and read 200 CSS variables per table on every key (now once per typing burst, only real rows); the pen guard joined the whole page's text on every key (now `doc.content.size`); the sheet-gap decorations were rebuilt for all blocks on every key (now built when the pushes change, mapped while typing). After: 6.6-7.8 ms per key (31 ms at 4x), long tasks 0-2 at 1x (39 at 4x, from 50); longest paste task 160 to 130 ms (1,012 to 667 ms at 4x). Scrolling was already smooth in this page (0.2 ms script per frame, frames 16.7 ms even at 4x). New CI budget `e2e/bigPageBudget.spec.ts`.
- **Why:** owner report (live site): big paste lags, scrolling and typing stutter.
- **Files:** src/paper/paginate.ts, src/editor/tableResize.ts, src/editor/penGuard.ts, e2e/bigPageBudget.spec.ts (new), docs/research/perf-big-pages-2026-11.md (new), docs/features/paper-page.md.

## 2026-11-09 (iPad fix 7: GoodNotes-style boxes, empty-box repair)

- **What:** (a) With the Lasso, hovering a box shows its frame; a tap on the frame or grip selects it (thin frame, six handles that change the width, grab the frame to move). (b) A floating bar under one selected box: text colour, size, font, Bold, Italic, align, list, Cut, Duplicate, Delete, ⋯ (the toolbar's own text operations applied to the whole box; switches to the Text tool first). (c) Shift-tap or the Lasso loop picks several boxes; dragging one moves them all in one write, one Undo ("n boxes moved"). (d) While dragging, the box is a see-through live copy with a thin red line at the landing spot; boxes it would cover are pushed down (and the push cascades) instead of overlapping; locked boxes and the default box are never moved. (e) Light snap guides (1.5 mm) to other boxes' edges and the page margins. (f) A box that crosses the gap between sheets is framed once per sheet, and one paragraph taller than a sheet is split at line boundaries for display (spacer widgets in paginate.ts), so no line sits in the gap. (g) Ctrl+A page-wide selection: Delete and Cut now empty every box (toast Undo). **Bug fix (live site):** an empty box lying on the default box (or on another box) showed two "Enter text" lines and could not be deleted. Pages are repaired on open (`repairBoxes`: only boxes with no text, no ink and only empty lines, within 12 mm of another box; empty orphan lines when the page has text); Backspace or Delete in an empty box removes it (toast Undo); Delete with a box selected removes it.

## 2026-11-09 (Paper: no text across the gap between sheets)

- **What:** Root cause: a box's text is one continuous flow and the sheets are only background rectangles with a 16 px gap, so lines ran straight over the gap and the bottom margin; a pasted plain-text list was also one block with line breaks. Fix: a ProseMirror extension (src/paper/paginate.ts) pushes whole top-level blocks that would reach a sheet's bottom margin, or start in it or in the gap, down to the top of the next sheet (margin-top as a node decoration, so the editor does not wipe it; BlockNote's 0.2 s margin transition is switched off in paper boxes). Several-line plain-text paste now makes one block per line so it can be paged line by line. Fit width and the reader are untouched. Test pastes 60 lines and checks no line touches a gap.
- **Files:** src/paper/paginate.ts (+ test), PaperFrame.tsx, src/editor/PageEditor.tsx, src/editor/clipboard.ts, src/index.css, e2e/paperPage.spec.ts, docs/features/paper-page.md

## 2026-11-08 (Fix #6: attention system)

- **What:** one banner spot at the top for important alerts. `app/attention/attentionLevel.ts` (pure: which problem, level, dismiss rules), `attentionSignals.ts` (update, storage, backups), `AttentionGate.tsx` (eager, tiny; waits for 2 s without typing) and lazy `AttentionBanner.tsx`. Level 1 locked pop-up unchanged (folded: the gate hands over to `LockedAlertGate`). Level 2 bright: Drive needs reconnecting (replaces `DriveBar`, deleted), Drive full, account not allowed, clock wrong, signed out, sync failing 3+ times or 30 min, backups failing twice, update needed. Level 3 calm: storage over 90%, finish sync setup, ordinary update (replaces `UpdateBar`; `UpdateRiskBar` stays). Dismiss: level 2 until next start or worse; level 3 for 7 days (`attn.snooze.<code>`). "Drive reconnected" toast when fixed. Text in `enO/hiO/esO`.

## 2026-11-08 (Fix #4: Drive pass renews itself)

- **What:** root cause in code: the pass was renewed only when a Drive call needed it (no renewal at start, on coming to the front or ahead of the end), a 401 gave up at once (no renew-and-retry), a blocked Google window could hang, and the only sign was a button hidden in Settings. Now: `drivePass.ts` renews 5 min before the end and shares one renewal; 401 renews and retries once; `keepDriveFresh` runs at start, on foreground, on online and each tick (quiet retry at most once a minute while a tap is needed); timeouts; if Google truly needs a tap, a top bar with one "Reconnect Drive" button (`DriveBar`); queued pictures resume after it.
- **Why:** owner-approved fix #4 (pictures did not sync after first setup on PC and iPad until Reconnect Drive + Sync now).

## 2026-11-08 (Fix #2: two devices, same line)

- **What:** (A) cause: a pulled batch read the local copy, wrote History (slow), then overwrote the block: a key saved in that gap was replaced and never sent, so the devices stayed apart until a refresh; also a block skipped because it was being typed in was never looked at again. Now the write is re-checked and decided again if the copy changed, and skipped blocks are retried. (B) a clash writes the other version on both devices' History and puts a "Changed on two devices" bar (Keep mine / Use other / Keep both) at the page; the choice syncs and the bar goes on both; Undo works for Use other / Keep both.
- **Why:** owner-approved fix #2.
- **Files:** `src/sync/{engine,clash}.ts`, `src/editor/{ClashBars,ClashBarsLazy,PageEditor,liveApply}`, `src/core/types.ts` (Block.clash), `src/share/checkRows.ts`, `src/i18n/{en,hi,es}O.ts`, tests `src/sync/engine.test.ts`, `e2e/syncClash.spec.ts`, `e2e/syncMock.ts` (shared mock server, now with a hold gate). First load 299.7 KB.

## 2026-11-08 (Fix #1: sign in first, new recovery code)

- **What:** (A) the welcome now opens with "Sign in with Google" or "Use without an account" (new step 0; asked once, existing users never see it). Sign in starts the Sync sign-in, then full-screen guide sheets (`onboarding/SignInGuide.tsx`): new account → passphrase → recovery code shown BIG with Print, Copy, Save as file (.txt) and last-four check; existing account → passphrase or code; "Not now" leaves the app usable. The welcome steps wait until the guide ends. (B) Settings › Sync › "Make a new recovery code" (signed in and unlocked): re-confirms the passphrase, locks the SAME master key with a fresh code, stores the new copy on the server first and checks it is there, then removes the old code copies; no re-encryption (`replaceRecoveryCode` in `sync/setup.ts`). New `deleteKeyBundle` on the record store (memory, shared test server, Supabase).
- **Why:** owner-approved fix #1.
- **Safety:** a failure before the new copy is stored leaves the old code working (unit-tested); a failed removal keeps both working and tells the person. The server may delete recovery copies only (migration `20261108000001_sync_bundle_delete.sql`, owner runs it once). Codes are never logged or stored by the app; only on screen, print, copy or the saved file.
- **Files:** `src/onboarding/{Welcome,SignInGuide,onboardingState}.tsx/ts`, `src/sync/{RecoveryCodeView,SyncSection,setup,syncService,LockedAlertGate}`, `src/sync/connectors/{types,memory,supabase}.ts`, `src/App.tsx`, `src/i18n/{en,hi,es}O.ts`, `supabase/migrations/20261108000001_sync_bundle_delete.sql`, tests `src/sync/recoveryCode.test.ts`, `e2e/signInFirst.spec.ts`, `e2e/welcome.spec.ts`, `e2e/syncLive.spec.ts`. First load 299.7 KB.

## 2026-11-08 (Automatic updates)

- **Updates apply by themselves.** A found version waits for a safe moment (nothing waiting to save, no pen or finger down, no Write pad draft, no dialog, and the app just came to the front or has been idle about 5 s), then flushes every save, takes the silent backup (R7-2), remembers the page and scroll, resets gestures and restarts. After the restart a toast says "Updated to the latest version" and the scroll is put back. Settings › General › "Update automatically" (on by default); off keeps the "Update available" bar. A question (Update anyway / Not now) shows only when data could be lost: the backup failed, storage is low, or a save keeps failing. R6-14 check frequency and the gesture reset are unchanged. Files: `app/autoUpdate.ts` (+ test), `app/updates.ts` (several listeners), `app/UpdateBar.tsx` (`UpdateRiskBar`), `editor/autosave.ts` (`hasPendingSaves`, `flushAllSaves`), `main.tsx`, `App.tsx`, `settings/builtinSettings.ts`, `i18n/{enO,esO,hiO}.ts`, `e2e/autoUpdate.spec.ts` (also on ipad-webkit). Rule 8: while ink is Lasso-selected a faint dashed line points to its linked line. Rule 5 was already how a Lasso move re-links (tested now). ## 2026-11-08 (R6-21 page jump after the lock toast; ipad-webkit red on the tap specs)

- **R6-21, real cause:** the toast moves nothing (it is fixed at the bottom); the page jumped 62 px when the toast's **Unlock** was tapped: that tap counted as a tap outside the open options pill (`app/popups.ts`), the pill closed, and its height left the stack above the page (the R6-8 hold only covers tool buttons and never works at scroll 0). Fix: the Toast carries `data-popup-trigger` like the ink pad, so a tap on a toast or its Undo/Unlock keeps the pill open and the page still. Proven by `e2e/lockToastStill.spec.ts` (chromium and ipad-webkit): the first line's screen position is the same before the toast, with it, after Unlock, and the next stroke lands at the same place. (Probe numbers: first line at y 474 through card, Add, Lock and the toast; 412 after Unlock; the scroller's top 208 → 146.)
- **ipad-webkit red (three specs), causes found in the CI traces:** (1) `gestureWatchdog` R7-6: the triple tap on "review" never reached the words. After the double tap on "summary" our selection's two drag handles sit on the next screen row, exactly over "review", so the taps grabbed a handle (the trace frame shows it). Test design, not the app: the double, triple and four-tap checks now run on fresh pages (each one still does the iPad sequence: a press whose lift is lost, away and back, then the taps). (2) `pencilTaps` "a slow second tap": the snapshot shows "Switched to Pencil mode · Undo": the automatic device-mode switch fired on the first pen tap (1.5 s quiet window after typing), which turns "tap twice" on mid-test, and the second tap was then the "activate" tap (cancelled). Not the iPad's state (it is in Pencil mode throughout): the tap specs now start with `advanced.autoSwitch` off. (3) `pencilTaps` "440 ms apart": 30 ms of headroom under the 450 ms tap window, with each tap a separate round trip to a loaded WebKit; the taps of both specs now run inside the page on its own clock (`tapRun`), so the gaps are exact. No timeout was widened. (4) `writePad` "Insert at the cursor, then Replace": Replace used a collapsed selection (received "…DNA reallyRNA"): the test set a DOM selection and focused the pad at once, but WebKit delivers `selectionchange` on its own task and the pad reads the page's selection when focused; the test now waits for the page to show the word as selected first.
- **Chromium `penTools` "double-tap the word, triple-tap the line" (red on main since the Round 7 bar commits):** reproduced locally with a probe. The first tap on "holds" came more than 1.5 s after the last key press, so the automatic switch to Pencil mode fired on it; it runs on `window` capture, so keyboardPolicy saw the same pointerdown already in Pencil mode with "tap twice" on and cancelled it as the "activate" tap, and the switch re-laid out the bars so the next two taps landed on the hint paragraph above the editor (the page had moved under them). With the switch off the triple tap selects the whole line. `e2e/mainBarHelpers.ts` `openNote` now starts every spec with `advanced.autoSwitch` off (a spec that wants it passes the key; `autoSwitch.spec.ts` sets its own), because a simulated pen tap in Touch mode flipping the mode mid-test is not an iPad state.
- **Also:** `useAutoDeviceMode`'s pointer-down counter joins the watchdog (a lost lift left it at 1 for good and the automatic switch never fired again).
- **Files:** `src/app/ui.tsx`, `src/settings/deviceModeSettings.ts`, `e2e/{gestureWatchdog,pencilTaps,writePad,lockToastStill}.spec.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`, docs.

## 2026-11-08 (Round 7 gesture watchdog: R7-1, R7-3, R7-4, R7-5, R7-6, R7-8; no Dexie version)

- **Root causes.** (1) A pointer that is down when the app goes to the background (another app or tab, the Update reload) never gets its `pointerup`/`pointercancel`. Everything that remembered "a pointer is down" then stayed stuck for good: the ink engine kept a half gesture (`controller.gesture`) and refused every later Pen, Eraser and Lasso stroke (`down()` and `lassoDown()` return while a gesture exists); a Lasso or handle gesture left the page `contenteditable=false` (`holdEditing`), so taps and the caret failed; a grip drag kept `gripDragging()` true (ink and Lasso ignore every pointer) and the page read-only; the selection guard's `pointerDown` stayed true, so every late collapse counted as the person's own and the double-tap word vanished (R7-4); the palm guard and penTap kept a Pencil "down". (2) The ink engine waited at most 120 frames (2 s) for the editor's container; a reload right after an update fetches the editor's files over the network, and the page then had no ink engine at all (R7-1). (3) When a new service worker takes over a page that is already running (Update pressed in another window, or the plugin's own reload missed), the old code kept running on the new version's files; nothing restarted it. (4) Live sync's `restoreCaret` put back a caret, never a range, after a synced block arrived: a selected word a few seconds old was lost.
- **Fixes.** New `app/gestureReset.ts`: one watchdog bus. On `visibilitychange` (both ways), `pageshow`, `pagehide`, window `focus` and `blur` every registered piece forgets its transient gesture state: `InkController.resetGestures` (a long half stroke is kept as writing, a stray touch dropped; erase finished; pen-down, double-tap memory, finger tap, hidden set, the page's editable hold and every pointer capture released; the pad armed again), `gripDrag.resetGripDrags`, penTap, palmGuard, `ownSelection.pointerDown`, tapSelect's counter, holdSelect's press, penGuard's `penState.down`, `selectGesture`. The pad also listens to `lostpointercapture` (the browser taking a pointer away ends that gesture, R7-3). `InkSurface` waits for `.bn-container` with a MutationObserver (no time limit). `updates.ts` `watchControllerChange` + `reloadWhenSafe`: a new controller on an already-controlled page restarts it at a safe moment (showing, no pen down, nothing unsaved; at the latest after 20 s); the Update button also resets gestures before restarting. `ownSelection.KEEP_MS` is 30 s (the keep ends at the next tap or key anyway; a late collapse with no input is always put back). `liveApply` keeps the selection as a range (anchor and head by block and offset) and only puts it back when the change moved it.
- **Debug dump.** Settings › Advanced › "Tool state › Copy tool state" (this device only; `app/toolStateReport.ts`): app build, visibility and focus, the tool, page mode, service-worker state, editable counts, the ink pad, and every registered piece's state (ink gesture, pen-down, held captures, pad pointer-events and touch-action, editable hold, selection counts, layers, tap and cancel counters; grip drag; palm guard; penTap; own-selection flags; tap counter; hold press; pen guard).
- **Tests.** Unit: `app/gestureReset.test.ts`, `app/updateRestart.test.ts`, `app/tools/gripDrag.test.ts` (jsdom), `editor/liveApply.test.ts` (a selected word survives a synced change). Browser (`e2e/gestureWatchdog.spec.ts`, chromium and ipad-webkit): Pen, Eraser and Lasso after a lost lift while hidden; a Lasso loop left open (page editable again, next loop selects); a bfcache `pageshow`; eraser after Pen, Lasso and a half handle drag; double and triple taps after a lost lift with the Text tool on; a double-tap selection still there after 5 s with sync live-apply applying another device's block.
- **Files:** `src/app/{gestureReset,toolStateReport,updates,penTap,palmGuard}.ts`, `src/app/tools/gripDrag.ts`, `src/main.tsx`, `src/ink/{controller.ts,InkSurface.tsx}`, `src/editor/{ownSelection,tapSelect,holdSelect,penGuard,gesture,liveApply}.ts`, `src/settings/labSettings.ts`, `src/i18n/{enO,esO,hiO}.ts`, tests above, `playwright.config.ts`, docs.

## 2026-11-07 (Pencil taps and Lasso words: R6-17..R6-20; no Dexie version)

- **What:** (1) **R6-18/R6-20 the double-tap selection stays.** Cause: after our word selection, iPadOS sent the second tap's own reaction (its double-tap selection, then the tap's synthetic mousedown/mouseup/click), whose mousedown collapsed the selection to a caret; it arrived inside the 0.7 s "recent input" window that `ownSelection.ts` treats as the person's own doing, so it was not put back. Fix: from the second tap on, `tapSelect.ts` cancels the Pencil's pointerdown and its stylus `touchstart` (no native double tap, no late click, no Scribble dot), and the selection it sets is kept for 1.5 s against any collapse that comes with no new tap or key (`keepSelection`); the mini toolbar ignores the pointerups that made the selection. (2) **R6-19 tap counts:** double = word, triple = the visual line on screen (caret positions at the row's left and right edges, `visualLineRange`), four taps = the paragraph; real iPad timing (450 ms between taps, 12 px drift; a press over 300 ms is a hold, not a tap). (3) **R6-17 Lasso T filter:** a loop (freeform or rectangle) around part of a paragraph selects the words whose centre is inside, first to last in reading order, as a text selection with the text menu (`editor/lassoWords.ts`, `InkController.wordsInRegion`); whole lines still select as blocks.
- **Why:** owner iPad notes R6-17..R6-20 (7 Nov). **Files:** `src/editor/{tapSelect,ownSelection}.ts`, new `src/editor/lassoWords.ts`, `src/ink/{controller.ts,InkSurface.tsx}`, `src/app/tools/main/MiniToolbar.tsx`; tests `src/editor/{tapSelect,ownSelection,lassoWords}.test.ts`, new `e2e/{pencilTaps,lassoWords}.spec.ts` (chromium and ipad-webkit), `e2e/penTapRules.spec.ts` (a pause before the caret-placing second tap), `playwright.config.ts`.
- **Tests:** unit 1479 pass (+7); e2e +8 (double tap stays 1 s through a forced collapse, 440 ms / 8 px still a double tap, triple = one screen row, four = paragraph, slow second tap = plain tap, Lasso words freeform and rectangle, whole line = block); the Lasso, ruler, magnifier, finger-scroll, paint-key and 500-stroke specs still pass. First load 279.1 KB (cap 320 KB).
- **Self-review:** not reproducible here: a real iPad's native double-tap gesture; cancelling the stylus touch is the same measure `penSelect.ts` uses under the Lasso, and the keep rule is the safety net if WebKit still sends the click. A quick tap-tap in one block under "tap twice" now selects the word rather than placing the caret (DECISIONS).

## 2026-11-07 (R6-13 real-time sync, R6-11 slow moments and sync scan fix, R6-2 AI keys on all devices; Dexie 22)

- **R6-13 Real-time sync.** (1) **Live apply:** blocks another device changed go into the open editor one by one, with no reload (`editor/liveApply.ts`, loaded only when sync is on): updated in place, inserted at their place, removed; the caret and selection stay (restored by block and text offset); nothing is an Undo step; the autosaver's snapshot takes them as saved so nothing is written back. A block being typed in is never touched: live apply waits 1.5 s after the last keystroke, and a block whose text in the editor differs from what was last saved keeps the typing (the newer change, as the merge rule says); the other device's text goes to the page's History and the "changed on two devices" notice shows. The engine announces what it applied (`sync/applied.ts`, also to other windows by BroadcastChannel). (2) **Realtime pings:** `sync/connectors/realtime.ts` opens a plain WebSocket to Supabase Realtime (Phoenix join, heartbeat every 25 s, a fresh sign-in token every 45 min, reconnect with a growing wait); it listens to one tiny row per account, `sync_pings`, which `sync_push` touches after each accepted save, so only "changed" and the server order travel. A ping starts a pull within about 150 ms; a ping at or below the order this device already saw (its own save) is ignored. Polling falls back to 10 s while the socket is closed and is a 60 s safety net while it is open; the feed closes when the app is hidden and reopens in front. Settings › Sync shows "Live" or "checking every 10 seconds". Migration `supabase/migrations/20261107000001_sync_realtime.sql` (owner steps at the top of owner-checks-pending.md); CSP `connect-src` adds `wss://…supabase.co`.
- **R6-11 Lag and freezes.** (1) **Record slow moments** (Settings › Advanced): long tasks (PerformanceObserver) and event-loop stalls (a 500 ms timer late by 250 ms or more, which also works in Safari) are kept on the device, the last 50 with route, tool, focused element and heap size; **Copy report** puts them on the clipboard as text. Nothing is sent anywhere; the recorder is a separate chunk loaded only when on. (2) **Real cause fixed:** `enqueueChanged` (the scan for edits made while tracking was off) ran on every sync, i.e. every 10 s and after every typing burst; it reads every row of every synced table, picture, book and recording bytes included (measured 41–68 ms for 5,000 blocks + 40 MB of pictures in Node's in-memory IndexedDB; on an iPad's disk-backed store the same scan reads all those bytes each time). It now runs once per app start (and when sync is set up or unlocked, as before); the outbox middleware tracks every save while the app runs. (3) Reviewed and left: the ink controller's MutationObserver re-measures block boxes on every keystroke when the page has strokes (O(blocks) rect reads, a few ms, no loop since the overlay is outside the observed editor element); the free bar's and the pill's `MutationObserver` on the root style are rAF-throttled and write no root style themselves; help tips add document listeners only while open.
- **R6-2 AI keys on all devices (opt-in).** Settings › AI › "Keep my AI keys on all my devices (locked with your sync passphrase)": the key list is mirrored into one `secrets` record with a fixed id (`ai/keySync.ts`, Dexie 22 table `secrets`, rule `record`), which sync locks with the master key like any record (the server cannot read it; the unit test checks the server state holds no key text). A pulled record replaces the local list; a local change writes the record; the switch travels in the record, so turning it off on one device turns it off everywhere and every device keeps the keys it already has; turning it on joins the record's keys with this device's. **Backups:** the `secrets` table is never written to a file (`NEVER_SAVED`), Replace keeps it, and version 22 is table-only so older files still replace.
- **Why:** owner approvals of 7 Nov (Round 6: R6-13, R6-11, R6-2). **Files:** `src/sync/{applied,engine,syncService,syncStatus,tables,SyncSection}.ts(x)`, `src/sync/connectors/{types,realtime,realtime.test,memory,index}.ts`, `src/editor/{liveApply,liveApply.test,pageSync,diff,mapping,PageEditor}.ts(x)`, `src/history/loserVersion.ts`, `src/perf/{slowMoments,slowMoments.test,slowMomentsSwitch}.ts`, `src/settings/labSettings.ts`, `src/main.tsx`, `src/ai/{keySync,keySync.test,keyStore,AiSection}.ts(x)`, `src/core/{db,types}.ts`, `src/share/{fullFile,fullFile.test,restore}.ts`, `src/core/migration.test.ts`, `src/i18n/{enO,esO,hiO}.ts`, `public/_headers`, `supabase/migrations/20261107000001_sync_realtime.sql`, `e2e/syncLive.spec.ts`, docs (sync.md, ai.md, CODE-MAP, LIMITATIONS, DECISIONS, HANDOFF, owner checks).
- **Tests:** unit `liveApply.test.ts` (5: update in place with caret kept, caret offset inside the changed block, insert and remove, typing wins with their text in History, echoes of own saves ignored), `realtime.test.ts` (5: address and join, ping parsing, live/heartbeat/stop, reconnect after a close, signed out), `keySync.test.ts` (4), `slowMoments.test.ts` (2); e2e `syncLive.spec.ts` (two browser contexts share one in-test server through a page binding and get pinged after each save: A's edit shows on B's open page without a reload, B's caret stays, B's typing reaches A). First load 341.8 KB.

- **What:** the owner's iPad findings and the approved Lasso design. **Lasso:** the ink pad now owns every pointer under the Lasso (no text drag-selection, block outline, Scribble or scroll under the ink; the page is not editable during a lasso or handle gesture; stylus touches are cancelled on the whole overlay, handles included; the overlay follows the page when a pill opens or closes), works over empty blocks and blank paper, filters default to ink only and each toggles (Pictures and Text select whole blocks: the editor's block selection alone, the mixed menu with ink; dragging moves the ink only, with a note), 60 % hit rule, eight handles (edge handles only on long sides), quarter-turn clicks, two-finger twist, a tap outside deselects, the menu is a row (Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Convert to text, Colour) plus ⋯ More (Delete, Thickness, Make card, Add to Summary, Name this drawing, Paste, Replay, Copy/Save as picture), the row chosen in the Lasso card. **Taps:** `ink/tapFilter.ts` drops quick tiny strokes and takes back the first mark of a double tap (tunable; DECISIONS); one tap counter (`tapSelect.ts`): Pencil double tap = word, triple = line in typing mode only, the Text tool stays; `penSelect.ts` no longer places a caret on pen down. **Ruler:** solid body, one pointer moves, two twist, 80 px, clicks every 15 degrees. **Magnifier:** full width, 30 % of the screen and resizable, 2.5x default, mirrors the page's ink (erase in step both ways, Eraser works in the pad), advance and wrap per the brief, a hint and a labelled draggable box, the pad-offset bug fixed (the pad size is measured from the element).

## 2026-11-06 (Ink redraw: leave the canvases alone when nothing moved)

- **Problem:** `pencil-webkit` (ipad-webkit) failed `inkLayers.spec.ts` "500 strokes draws within the budget" on about half of the runs since a29ff83 (81 to 163 ms a frame against 80), even with the median of five rounds. The deploy of all of Wave 3 waited on it.
- **Cause:** every layout check (`dirty()`: a resize, any editor DOM mutation such as a keystroke, a ResizeObserver tick) ran a full `redraw`: three screen-sized canvases cleared, the layer bitmap put back, the three canvas layers (one with `mix-blend-mode: multiply`) composited again. The strokes themselves were already cached in a bitmap (no per-stroke work). In Chromium that frame is 1 to 3 ms; in CI's software-rendered WebKit, at the iPad's 2x canvases on a shared 2-core runner, it is about 80 to 130 ms, so the test always sat at the edge of its budget. A probe (branches `diag/ink-budget-head` and `diag/ink-budget-live`, Chromium) shows the per-frame work is identical at a29ff83 and at HEAD: 6 `getBoundingClientRect`, 3 `clearRect`, 1 `drawImage`, 0 stroke fills. So no Wave 3 code slowed the redraw; the budget was never safe in WebKit.
- **Fix:** `InkController.redraw` now keeps a key of what the canvases show (doc version, layout version, view top and size, dpr, hidden strokes, night, tool) and skips `draw` and `drawLive` when it is unchanged and nothing is live (no gesture, no eraser ring, no peeled tape). A resize or keystroke with nothing moved no longer touches the canvases; a real change paints as before. On the iPad this also removes a full canvas repaint per keystroke while ink is on the page.
- **Test:** the budget test keeps its 80 ms limit and now also checks that the measured resize rounds made no `clearRect` call (the canvases were left alone).
- **CI:** GitHub Actions jobs are refused: "recent account payments have failed or your spending limit needs to be increased" (the repo is private; the free minutes are used up). Until the owner fixes Billing & plans, no CI runs and no deploy, whatever the code. The two diagnostic PRs (#2, #3) were closed unrun; their branches stay for a WebKit probe later.
- **Files:** `src/ink/controller.ts`, `e2e/inkLayers.spec.ts`, docs.

## 2026-11-06

- **What:** Redesign step 3, the shared ink engine. **Drawing:** Pen (five types: Fountain, Ball, Brush, Pencil, Marker; thickness, colour, pressure, tilt, sharpness, flatness, stabilisation, thickness by speed, dashed or dotted), Highlighter (behind the ink, opacity; "snap to text lines" turns a stroke over words into a real text highlight; Save to notes keeps the passage in a "Highlights" page; Highlight eraser), Eraser (Precise, Standard, Whole stroke, size, highlights only), pen gestures (scribble to erase, circle to lasso), draw and hold for perfect shapes (line, rectangle, circle or ellipse, triangle, arrow; snap and fill). Pointer events with coalesced and predicted points, a low-latency canvas, perfect-freehand outlines, raw points saved (x, y, pressure, tilt, time) with a start time per stroke. **Lasso on ink:** free-hand or rectangle; move, resize and turn with separate handles; duplicate, colour, delete, copy or save a PNG; keyboard door to select and delete strokes. **Layers:** up to 12 per page, name, show or hide, lock, reorder, delete with Undo, paper behind the ink (blank, lined, grid, dots); ink is anchored to the block it was drawn over and follows reflow; a "View as drawn" stub for the reader. **One history:** Undo and Redo walk ink and text together. **Data:** Dexie version 13 (tables `inkLayers`, `inkChunks`, indexed by page); History copies, "Everything" and "Notes only" files carry ink; Delete forever erases it; Replace now accepts files from database version 12. **Speed:** three canvases that cover only the visible part, a cached bitmap per layer, only the active stroke redrawn. **Palm rejection:** finger ignored while the Pencil is down; "Finger can draw" Automatic, Always, Never. The ribbon's Draw tools run the engine. New dependency perfect-freehand 1.2.3 (MIT, no dependencies, 4.5 KB source, no production audit findings). Help tips and Hindi and Spanish texts added.
- **Why:** the owner approved the redesign (step 3 of 10): nothing drew until now.

- **What:** Batch 3a, urgent data-safety fixes from the owner's real iPad Pro (Safari app, Pencil Pro, Scribble on). **1. A Pencil drag over text deleted it.** Most likely cause: iPadOS Scribble reads a Pencil stroke over editable text as handwriting or scratch-out, and the change can arrive as `delete*`, `insertReplacementText`, a composition, or a plain DOM change with no `beforeinput`; the 2f guard only looked at `delete*` for 300 ms, and the stroke was still given to the browser. This cannot be proven without the iPad. Fix, in layers: (a) new `penSelect.ts`: a Pencil stroke that starts on text is handled by us. The pointer events are cancelled, Safari's stylus touchstart/touchmove are cancelled (a finger still scrolls; CSS `touch-action` cannot tell pen from finger), the page is made not editable for the length of the stroke so Scribble has nothing to write in, the selection is set from the Pencil position (`posAtCoords`) in ProseMirror and the browser, then the page is editable and focused again. A double tap selects a word. A stroke that reaches a picture is left to `dragSelect.ts`. (b) `penGuard.ts`: the `beforeinput` guard now also refuses writing (insertText, replacement, composition) while a Pencil gesture that began in the page is active or ended less than 500 ms ago; a ProseMirror `filterTransaction` rejects any transaction that removes or replaces content in that window (Undo/Redo and real key presses are exempt; taps on the bar or menus begin outside the page text, so Delete from them still works). (c) New setting Settings › Advanced › **Pencil writes text (Scribble)**, off by default, with a Scribble note; on = the browser handles the Pencil as before. **2. Undo/Redo and lost pictures.** Findings: picture files are never deleted (blocks are soft-deleted and pictures stay in `images`; no cleanup code exists), so a restored picture block always finds its file. The real gaps were: History took no copy before a big delete (a copy is made at most every 5 minutes, so a delete soon after one could not be recovered); the bar's greyed Undo state could be stale; and the bar commands depended on editor focus. Fixes: `pageSync.ts` forces a History copy of the saved page before saving a delete of 3 or more blocks or of any picture block (`isBigDelete`); new `historyActions.ts` runs Undo/Redo on the editor view itself and then focuses it (bar, ribbon, control panel and the Undo messages use it); the bar re-reads its Undo/Redo state every 400 ms.
- **Why:** Owner's iPad Pro test after batch 3.
- **Files:** new `src/editor/penSelect.ts`, `historyActions.ts`, `e2e/undoPictures.spec.ts`; changed `penGuard.ts` (+ test), `pageSync.ts` (+ test), `PageEditor.tsx`, `DockedBar.tsx`, `src/app/tools/editorCommands.ts`, `src/app/reading/ReadingSelection.tsx`, `src/settings/builtinSettings.ts`, `src/i18n/` (en, es7, hi7), `e2e/penSelect.spec.ts`; docs `editor-and-blocks.md`, `device-modes.md`, `LIMITATIONS.md`, `DECISIONS.md`, `HANDOFF.md`.
- **Tests:** unit tests for the pen rules (input and transaction) and `isBigDelete`; e2e with simulated pen pointers: Scribble beforeinput events (delete, cut, replacement, composition, insertText) and a direct DOM change during and just after a pen drag leave the text unchanged and the text selected; with the Scribble setting on the browser stays in charge; a key press still deletes; text plus pictures deleted, Undo from the bar brings them back drawn, Redo deletes again, History restores them; Undo/Redo with focus on the bar; a delete of 4 blocks is in History even right after an earlier copy.
