- **What:** Batch 3: the privacy lock and Reading mode. **Lock:** a page, notebook or database can be locked (page ⋯ menu, notebook Tab options, database ⋯ menu, ribbon File › Protect); the lock screen replaces the content, which is never loaded or drawn. Ways to unlock, chosen in the new Settings › Lock: Face ID / Touch ID / Windows Hello (WebAuthn platform passkey, `userVerification: required`, only the credential id kept on the device), a 4–6 digit PIN and a password (salted PBKDF2-SHA-256 hashes in the `settings` table, so backups carry the lock settings). Plain words everywhere: a privacy lock, not encryption. Forgot: device check, or a ticked and honest "Remove the lock". It locks again on leaving, on hide or close (the screen is blanked at once) and after 5 idle minutes (1–30 in Settings). Hidden from search (title with a 🔒 only), Review, Recent, Favourites, sidebar text and Calendar. **Reading mode:** the 📖 Read part of the switch turns the page into Reading mode in place (Read tools, progress with minutes left, ruler, panels) or opens a full-screen reader (controls on a tap in the middle, edge taps and swipes turn pages in paged mode); chosen in Settings › Reading, both always available. Themes (Same as the app, Day, Night, Sepia, Paper, Dark contrast), fonts (incl. OpenDyslexic), size, line spacing, margin, width, scroll or pages, line focus or ruler, resume and bookmarks per page and device, an outline of the headings, highlight while reading (saved, with Undo), Lock to reading, "always open in Reading" for a page or notebook, read aloud with the Web Speech API (word marks, speed 0.5–2×, device voices, read from a tap, on into the next page, sleep timer 10/20/30/60), blanks softly marked with a Self-test switch. Extras: reading streak, Look up a word, keep the screen awake. The Read set tools and the ribbon View items that said "coming" are real; the control panel lists them.

- **What:** Batch 2f, urgent fix. The owner's iPad showed no short bar, no ribbon and no Show tool bar button. (B) **Data loss guard for the Pencil** (`src/editor/penGuard.ts`): a `beforeinput` that deletes while the Pencil is down or in the 300 ms after it lifts is refused unless a key was pressed in the last 500 ms; if 20 or more characters still vanish right after pen input, a message offers Undo. The pen drag selection code (`dragSelect.ts`) only sets a selection and never deletes (tested). Not done: preventDefault on pointer or touch events during a pen drag, because that also stops the browser's own text selection and scrolling; the cause (iPadOS Scribble scratch-out) could not be proven without the real iPad. (1) **Write · Draw · Read switch** on the docked bar (icon-only, three parts) that changes by itself (pen → Draw, typing or text focus → Write; Reading mode will → Read) and a pin "Keep this set"; each set has its own tools, saved per device; Edit bar customises each set (tick, reorder, drag, add any tool, reset). (2) **Short bar restyled** "Apple-like calm": rounded translucent pill with blur (solid fallback), thin Lucide icons (emoji and text icons replaced, a clear eraser), 44 px targets, gentle shadow, AA contrast in Day, Night and Sepia, reduced motion respected; **All tools** button with icon and words (opens the Toolbar menu until the ribbon) and a search icon. Default tools: Write = Bold, Italic, Colors, Highlight, Bulleted list, Checklist, Picture, More; Draw and Read as planned, "coming" ones dimmed with a reason. (3) **Control panel** (Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+K, bar icon, page ⋯ menu, Settings): searchable list of every tool and setting, grouped like the ribbon, with place, shortcut, run and Pin to bar; one registry file (`toolRegistry.ts`). (4) **Device picker**: Phone, iPad / tablet, Computer / laptop (+ Apple Pencil), preselected, first start and once for existing users, also in Settings. (5) **Sidebar**: Recent (last 8, folded), Favourites (star from ⋯ menu or row), Calendar shortcut and a new **Calendar screen** (dated database rows and journal days, month and week). Page ⋯ menu: Add to favourites, Search all features, Show/Hide the tool bar. New dependency lucide-react 1.60.0 (ISC, audit unchanged, bundled per icon).
- **Why:** Owner-approved plan from the ribbon research and mockup (2026-11-04).
- **Files:** `src/app/tools/` (new: registry, runtime, state, icons, switch, tool button, control panel, device picker), `src/app/favourites.ts`, `QuickPages.tsx`, `pageTitle.ts`, `src/databases/CalendarScreen.tsx`, `calendarEntries.ts`, `views/calendarData.ts`, `src/editor/DockedBar.tsx`, `EditBarPanel.tsx`, `PageEditor.tsx`, `src/App.tsx`, `src/app/router.ts`, `keys.ts`, `deviceMode.ts`, `ui.tsx`, `src/notebooks/Sidebar.tsx`, `src/pages/PageTree.tsx`, `PageView.tsx`, `src/settings/DeviceSection.tsx`, `src/help/helpRegistry.ts`, `src/i18n/` (en, hi7, es7), `package.json`, `playwright.config.ts`, tests (unit: toolRegistry, toolbarState, controlRows, deviceKind, favourites, calendarEntries, router; e2e: toolBar, controlPanel, devicePicker, sidebarQuick; updated deviceModes, autoSwitch, topBar, helpers), docs `toolbar-ribbon.md`, `control-panel.md`, `device-modes.md`, `navigation.md`, `keyboard-shortcuts.md`, `HELP-TEXTS.md`, `research/toolbar-ribbon.md`.
- **Self-review:** All of it was tested in Chrome with made-up pen events. The default bar changed (Underline, Key point, Make card and New line are no longer on it; they are in More and Edit bar). Real Pencil and iPad feel need the owner. Calendar reads rows by the databaseId index and cuts the date range in memory (no database upgrade); fine to 10,000 rows in the speed test.

- **What:** Batch 2c, the owner's iPad feedback. (1) Touch and Pencil: the docked bar is always visible at the top (Pencil moved from the bottom); the iPad is now recognised as touch (Safari presents it as a Mac), with a one-time repair for devices that were given Computer; sticky section chips now really stick, below the bar. (2) New ⋯ Toolbar menu on the bar: every tool and action, copy and paste items, Edit bar; 44 px rows, arrows and Esc, focus returns to the button. (3) Auto switch (Settings › Advanced: Pencil only, Both ways, Off) replaces the one-time Pencil offer: a pen switches to Pencil with a message and Undo, a finger can switch back; it waits for typing and gestures to finish and never flips back and forth. (4) Own colour button and picker for the bar: outlined swatch showing text and highlight colour, named colours, borders and text pass WCAG AA in Day, Night and Sepia; every bar button checked in Night. (5) Copy as plain text, Copy as Markdown, Copy page with pictures (HTML with data: pictures plus text; text fallback), Paste as plain text (menu and Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+V); pasted web HTML is cleaned; each copy says Copied. Normal copy and paste verified for formatting, links and pictures on the same page, another page and another notebook. The ribbon request was deferred to the next batch (DECISIONS).
- **Why:** The owner's iPad showed the old floating toolbar, a bar that was hard to see in dark mode, and no easy copy and paste options.

## 2026-11-03

- **What:** Batch 2b: three device modes (Computer, Touch, Pencil) and a docked formatting bar. First run picks Computer or Touch from the device; the Pencil is offered once on the first pen touch and never switched on silently. One-tap switch in Settings › General and the page ⋯ menu; Settings › Advanced has each preset value (bar position, size, button tips, shortcut hints, hide when idle). Touch and Pencil show a docked bar (sticky under the top bar, or fixed at the bottom above the keyboard) with the text style menu, Bold, Italic, Underline, Key point, Make card, ↵ New line, More and Done; More holds every other old toolbar action; Edit bar pins, unpins, reorders (drag and ↑ ↓), sets size, position, auto-hide and Resets, saved per device. Done and a tap on empty space clear the selection; ↵ New line and a tap area under the last block add or focus an empty last paragraph. A physical keyboard turns shortcut hints on even in Touch. Pencil test page: a tap draws a dot (pen and highlighter), stylus touchstart/move/end are stopped at once, double-tap and gestures are blocked, prediction line on by default, pressure rescaled to the pen's own range, smoothed lines, and Copy results falls back to a selected text box.
- **Why:** The floating toolbar is awkward on an iPad (covers text, hover-only tips, no way to start a new line without a keyboard); the Pencil page felt laggy and missed taps.

The `onChange` hook on device settings is new shared code (settings registry), so the full tests ran. The ⋯ menu grew to 8 items (two mode switches). Choices made alone are in DECISIONS (11 rows).

- **What:** Added the throwaway Pencil test page `/pencil-test.html` (`public/pencil-test.html`, `.css`, `.js`): a full-screen canvas that draws only with a pen (fingers ignored), with pen, highlighter, eraser (rubs out strokes), paper styles (blank, lined, grid, dotted), colour and width, an own-prediction line, live numbers (pointer type, pressure, tilt, altitude/azimuth, events per second, coalesced and predicted events, frame rate, low-latency canvas) and a Copy results button (clipboard, with a text-box fallback). Open saved file asks: Replace my notes (safety copy first in `learning-notes-before-restore`, one transaction, dark bar and Undo restore) or Add as copies; old JSON files still open as copies. Password lock works in chunks for the zip; old locked files still open. Screen reordered: Save, Restore, Share one notebook, Before last update, More. Help tips, docs and tests updated.

- **What:** The # menu reads tags from memory, so its list keeps up with typing, and Enter on a list that is behind makes the tag from what was actually typed. Text that cannot be a tag is put back. Both Chromium and WebKit now pass at full typing speed.

- **What:** A second window on the same page is read-only, with a notice and Edit here, which takes the page over and reloads what was saved. The first window saves its last changes and turns read-only. Uses the browser's free Web Locks; a browser without them edits as before.
- **Why:** Audit B7: two windows silently overwrote each other, and could make the order-key tie on one device.
- **Files:** src/editor/pageLock.ts, src/editor/PageEditor.tsx, src/i18n/, e2e/twoWindows.spec.ts, docs/LIMITATIONS.md, docs/features/editor-and-blocks.md

- **What:** The update offer has its own small bar, apart from toasts. Messages no longer replace it, it no longer replaces an Undo, and × hides it until the next app start. The `stay` toast option is gone. Also: the file check now reaches the editor format through search/searchText.ts, so no new feature link was added.
- **Why:** Audit B10: one toast slot made the offer and Undo overwrite each other.
- **Files:** src/app/UpdateBar.tsx, src/app/updates.ts, src/app/appUi.ts, src/app/ui.tsx, src/App.tsx, src/search/searchText.ts, src/share/checkRows.ts, docs/features/offline-and-install.md

- **What:** Importing checks every row field by field, refuses duplicate ids, bad order keys and text the editor cannot draw, and copies known fields only, so purgedAt from a file is never kept. The whole import runs in one transaction, so a failed import leaves nothing, not even tags. Every failure now shows a message. A link cell from a file only opens if it passes the same check as a typed link.
- **Why:** Audit B6 and S4: a damaged file could break every save on a page, or fail with no message.
- **Files:** src/share/checkRows.ts, src/share/bundle.ts, src/share/importBundle.ts, src/share/DataScreen.tsx, src/core/order.ts, src/databases/views/TableView.tsx, src/i18n/, e2e/sharing.spec.ts, docs/features/backup-and-files.md

- **What:** Backup now says what is and is not included. A saved file over 200 MB shows a warning that it could not be opened again. A notebook file reads only the pictures it uses.

- **Files:** src/share/DataScreen.tsx, src/share/bundle.ts, src/i18n/, docs/features/backup-and-files.md

- **What:** The upgrade backup file now holds pictures as base64 text, and reading such a file brings them back. Moved base64.ts to core/ so the backup code can use it.

- **What:** A failed save stays pending: it retries after 2 s, 4 s, 8 s up to a minute and on every flush. The changes are kept in localStorage, also when the page is left, and a lasting Not saved yet notice with Try again shows until they are in the database. Kept changes are no longer thrown away when bringing them back fails.
- **Why:** Audit B2: a failed save was never retried on leaving, so typing vanished without a message.

Why: the audit in docs/plans/build-diagnosis-2026-11-14.md section 7 found contradicting rules and a plan that had been overridden without being rewritten. No app code changed. The build stays PAUSED.
